- `range(5)` generates the sequence: `0, 1, 2, 3, 4`
- `x**2` squares each value of `x`
- The square brackets `[]` make it a **list comprehension**, which collects all the results into a new list

**Step-by-step evaluation:**
- `0**2 = 0`
- `1**2 = 1`
- `2**2 = 4`
- `3**2 = 9`
- `4**2 = 16`

**Final output:**
```python
[0, 1, 4, 9, 16]
